Now, global seed generation and optimization plan in detail.

Pseudo code:

```
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

_OBJ = {}
_GLOBAL_BEST = {'score': np.inf, 'seq': None}

def next_pow2(m): return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def self_conv(a):
    n=len(a); m=2*n-1; N=next_pow2(m)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    return np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:m]

def cross_lag(a,p):
    n=len(a); N=next_pow2(2*n-1) # or max(2*n-1,?) p length 2n-1
    ar=a[::-1]
    FA=np.fft.rfft(ar,N); FP=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(FA*FP,N)
    return conv[n-1:2*n-1]

def compute_score(a):
    S=a.sum()
    if S<1e-300: return np.inf
    c=self_conv(a)
    return 2.0*len(a)*c.max()/(S*S)

def normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.maximum(a,0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-300: return np.full_like(a,1.0/a.size)
    return a/s

def softmax_from_y(y, fix):
    n=len(y)+1
    m=max(0.0, float(np.max(y)))
    yc=np.clip(y-m, -80.0, 0.0)
    e=np.exp(yc)
    wfix=np.exp(-m)
    denom=wfix+e.sum()
    a=np.empty(n)
    if fix>0: a[:fix]=e[:fix]/denom
    a[fix]=wfix/denom
    if fix+1<n: a[fix+1:]=e[fix+1:]/denom
    return a

def y_from_a(a, fix):
    a=normalize(a)
    afix=a[fix]
    if afix<=0: a = a + 1e-300; normalize; afix=a[fix]
    y=np.empty(len(a)-1)
    if fix>0: y[:fix]=np.log(np.maximum(a[:fix],1e-300)/afix)
    if fix+1<len(a): y[fix:]=np.log(np.maximum(a[fix+1:],1e-300)/afix)
    return np.clip(y,-80,80)
```

But if a[fix] huge or small? choose median. Let's choose fix_idx = int(np.argmax(a))? Then a_fix max, y<=0. Good. But as optimization, fixing max may not stay max; y can go positive up to clip. Bound in objective with m handles. Initial y max 0. Good.

Objective:

```
def objective_wrapper(x):
    beta=_OBJ['beta']; fix=_OBJ['fix']
    return lse_objective(x,beta,fix)

def lse_objective(y,beta,fix):
    a=softmax_from_y(y,fix)
    c=self_conv(a)
    cmax=float(c.max())
    shifted=beta*(c-cmax)
    shifted -= shifted.max()
    exps=np.exp(shifted)
    s=exps.sum()
    val=cmax + np.log(s)/beta
    p=exps/s
    ga=2.0*cross_lag(a,p)
    T=float(np.dot(ga,a))
    # grad_y = a_j*(ga_j - T) for excluded j
    g=np.empty(len(y))
    if fix>0:
       seg=a[:fix]*(ga[:fix]-T); g[:fix]=seg
    if fix+1<len(a):
       g[fix:]=a[fix+1:]*(ga[fix+1:]-T)
    return val, g
```

This uses two large FFTs per eval: self_conv and cross_lag. Could optimize by computing c and p and grad in one? cross needs p. Fine.

Can avoid computing c exact max if beta smooth? Need c for grad. Good.

Exact score from y: `a=softmax; compute_score(a)`.

Now `optimize_log`:
```
def optimize_log(a0, time_limit, beta_schedule, report_func=None, exact_every=...):
    a=normalize(a0); n=len(a); fix=int(np.argmax(a)); y=y_from_a(a,fix)
    best_score=score(a); best_a=a.copy(); best_y=y.copy()
    # estimate fun time: set _OBJ? Call objective 3 times with current y and some beta
    est = estimate_time(a, fix, beta_schedule[0])
    for beta in beta_schedule:
       if time.monotonic() > time_limit - 5: break
       remaining = time_limit - time.monotonic()
       if remaining < 20: break
       maxfun = int(remaining*0.35 / max(est,1e-6))
       maxfun = max(300, min(maxfun, 20000))
       _OBJ['beta']=beta; _OBJ['fix']=fix
       res = minimize(objective_wrapper, y.copy(), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True,
                      bounds=[(-80,80)]*(n-1),
                      options={'maxfun':maxfun,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-8,'maxls':20})
       a_new=softmax_from_y(res.x,fix)
       sc=score(a_new)
       if sc < best_score:
          best_score=sc; best_a=a_new; best_y=res.x; print...
       # update y to best_y maybe if no improvement but res.x exact slightly? Use res.x if val lower? We can if exact not worse much? using exact best.
       if not improved and beta > some: maybe continue? 
    return best_a,best_score
```

Bounds list 12287 * tuples memory ~200k, okay but creation cost. Can pass `bounds=(-80,80)`? scipy accepts tuple for all? I think bounds can be sequence, or if a single sequence? For L-BFGS-B, bounds list of tuples; passing a single tuple may be interpreted as one bound? Better create list: `[(-80.0,80.0)] * (n-1)` creates references to same tuple okay. Memory pointers ~n*8 ~100KB plus tuple. Fine.

Estimate time: We can do:
```
def estimate_fun_time(y, fix, beta):
    _OBJ['beta']=beta; _OBJ['fix']=fix
    t0=time.perf_counter()
    for _ in range(3): objective_wrapper(y)
    return (time.perf_counter()-t0)/3
```
But objective_wrapper uses global and computes. Use before setting reduce? Good.

Now, targeted line search after L-BFGS:
```
def peak_refine(a, n_steps, budget, rng):
    a=normalize(a); best=score(a)
    for it in range(n_steps):
       c=self_conv(a); cmax=c.max(); k=int(np.argmax(c))
       # active top K weights: take top 64, p = c_top / sum? maybe
       order=np.argsort(c)[-K:]; p=zeros; p[order]=c[order]**2? But if top equal.
       grad=2*cross_lag(a,p)
       # line search step in a with projection
       d=-grad
       gnorm=norm(d)
       base=min(0.5, 0.1*best? /gnorm?)
       for mult in [0.25,0.5,1,2]:
          a_try=proj_simplex(a+mult*base*d)
          sc=score(a_try)
          if sc<best-1e-12:
             best=sc; a=a_try; break
       if no improve and it%20==0: a=normalize(a*(1+0.01 rng))
    return a,best
```
`proj_simplex` expensive sorting each try; n ~8000, few steps okay. Implement efficient:
```
def proj_simplex(v):
    n=len(v); u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u*(np.arange(1,n+1))+1>cssv)[0][-1]
    theta=cssv[rho]/(rho+1)
    return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
```
This enforces sum rho+1. But sum after subtract may not exactly 1 due round; divide by sum. For v with large negative? Our a positive, gradient maybe; d negative. Good.

Maybe use y-space line search instead of proj: `y_try = y - lr grad_y`, no proj. But peak gradient for exact max in y is grad_y. We can line search in y, likely cheaper. Let's implement `peak_refine_y(y,fix,beta?,K)`. But simple.

However, L-BFGS on LSE already does similar. Peak refine can handle exact max with top active weights.

`active_gradient_top(a, K)`: c; get top K indices; weights p = c[order]^(gamma)? Use p = exp(beta_active*(c-cmax)) with beta_active high? Simpler p uniform on top K. This gives subgradient of sum of top K, encourages flatten. Use K=32. `grad=2*cross_lag(a,p)`.

Then parameterization y line search: Given y, compute a, grad_y. For factor m, y_try = y - lr*grad_y (clipped bounds). Evaluate score. This avoids projection. Since step in y; scale of grad_y depends on a values. Need adaptive lr. Use backtracking maybe:
- Start lr = 1e-3 / (norm(grad_y)+1e-12)? But y gradient variable. For active top, maybe.

```
def central_seed(n, reverse=False):
    a=np.empty(n)
    a[0]=1.0
    for k in range(n-1): a[k+1]=a[k]*(2*k+1)/(2*k+2)
    if reverse: a=a[::-1]
    return normalize(a)
```
For n=12288, computing O(n) fine.

```
def power_seed(n, el, er):
    x=np.arange(n)+0.5
    xx=x/(n)
    a=(xx+1e-6)**el * ((1-xx+1e-6)**er)
```

def low_res_search(deadline, rng, parent, max_time=180):
   n=256  # maybe 384? 256 fast
   t0=time.monotonic()
   end=min(deadline, t0+max_time)

   best_low=scored[0][1]; best_score=scored[0][0]

   for i in range(min(5,len(scored))):

       slot=min(remaining, remaining*share/sum_remaining) ??? dynamic.

    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()

def as_nonneg(x):
    return np.maximum(np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64), 0.0)

def normalize_sequence(a): ...

def self_conv(a): ...

def cross_lag(a,p): ... # p length should be 2n-1; if p length different? We can pad/truncate: 
   n=len(a); if len(p)<2*n-1: p=np.pad(p,(0,2*n-1-len(p))); else p=p[:2*n-1]
   N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
   ar=a[::-1]
   FA=np.fft.rfft(ar,N); FP=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
   conv=np.fft.irfft(FA*FP,N)

```
def update_best_sequence(sc, seq, label):
    if not np.isfinite(sc): return
    if seq is None: return
    s=np.asarray(seq, dtype=float).ravel()
    if s.size ==0: return

        sc = np.inf

        _GLOBAL['best_score']=float(sc); _GLOBAL['best_seq']=s.copy(); print
```

Let's detail each function code.

`normalize_sequence`:
```
def normalize_sequence(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0:
        return np.ones(1)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        return np.ones_like(a, dtype=np.float64)/a.size
    return a/s
```
If a contains inf, max(inf) sum inf -> fallback uniform? Use `np.nan_to_num` first:
`a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)` then clip. Good.

`resample_sequence`:
```
def resample_sequence(seq, n):
    seq=normalize_sequence(seq)
    m=seq.size
    if m==n: return seq
    if m==1: return np.full(n, seq[0]/n)
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    y=np.interp(x_new, x_old, seq)
    return normalize_sequence(y)
```

`central_seed`:
```
def central_seed(n, reverse=False):
    a=np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    a[0]=1.0
    for k in range(n-1):
       a[k+1]=a[k]*(2.0*k+1.0)/(2.0*k+2.0)
    if reverse: a=a[::-1].copy()
    return normalize_sequence(a)
```
For n=0? no.

`power_seed`:
```
def power_seed(n, el, er):
    x=np.linspace(1e-4,1.0-1e-4,n)
    a=(x+1e-6)**el * ((1.0-x)+1e-6)**er + 1e-8
    return normalize_sequence(a)
```
With negative exponents, x min 1e-4, value up to 100, fine.

`random_fourier_seed`:
```
def random_fourier_seed(n, rng, harm=4):
    t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    vals=0.8 + 0.1*rng.standard_normal(n).cumsum()? no. Use base 0.8 + 0.2? Need positive.
    base=0.6 + 0.1*rng.uniform(size=n)
    for m in range(1,harm+1):
       amp=rng.uniform(0.05,0.25)/m
       phase=rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*np.pi)
       use_sin = bool(rng.integers(0,2)) # no if? allowed? It's function bool. But if use Sin.
       if use_sin:
          vals += amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*t + phase)
       else:
          vals += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*t + phase)
    vals=np.maximum(vals,1e-3)
    return normalize_sequence(vals)
```
Using `if use_sin:` fine.

`random_mixture_seed`:
```
def random_mixture_seed(n, rng, K):
    t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    centers=np.sort(rng.uniform(0.0,1.0,K))
    widths=rng.uniform(0.01,0.30,K)
    weights=rng.dirichlet(np.ones(K)*0.5)
    a=np.full(n,1e-4)
    for c,w,sw in zip(centers,widths,weights):
       a += sw*np.exp(-0.5*((t-c)/max(w,1e-3))**2)
    return normalize_sequence(a)
```

`random_lowpass_seed`:
```
def random_lowpass_seed(n, rng):
    c=np.exp(1j*rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi,n))
    amp=np.exp(-np.linspace(0.0,4.0,n))
    spec=np.zeros(n, complex)
    spec[:]=amp*c
    # ensure Hermitian for real inverse? rfft? Use ifft and take real.
    a=np.real(np.fft.ifft(spec))
    # add small positive and maybe reflect? We need nonnegative; clip.
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    if a.sum()<1e-12: a=np.ones(n)*0.1
    a = a + 0.01
    return normalize_sequence(a)
```
This may produce periodic, endpoints not zero. Fine.

`make_seeds`:
```
def make_seeds(n, rng, parent_seq=None, extra_seq=None):
    seeds=[]
    if parent_seq is not None and parent_seq.size>0:
       a=resample_sequence(parent_seq,n); seeds.append(("parent",a))
    if extra_seq is not None and extra_seq.size>0:
       a=resample_sequence(extra_seq,n); seeds.append(("extra",a))
    a=central_seed(n,False); seeds.append(("central",a))
    a=central_seed(n,True); seeds.append(("rev_central",a))
    for el,er in [(0.0,0.0),(0.5,0.0),(0.0,0.5),(0.5,0.5),(1.0,0.0),(0.0,1.0),(-0.25,0.0),(0.0,-0.25),(0.25,0.25)]: seeds.append((f"pow{el},{er}", power_seed(n,el,er)))
    for i in range(8):
       harm=int(rng.integers(2,7))
       a=random_fourier_seed(n,rng,harm)
       seeds.append((f"fourier{i}",a))
    for k in [5,10,20,40]:
       a=random_mixture_seed(n,rng,k)
       seeds.append((f"mix{k}",a))
    for i in range(4):
       a=random_lowpass_seed(n,rng)
       seeds.append((f"lowpass{i}",a))
    # current global best if different length? 
    best_seq = _GLOBAL.get('best_seq')
    if best_seq is not None and best_seq.size>0:
       seeds.append(("current_best", resample_sequence(best_seq,n)))
    return seeds
```
Could be many seeds ~31. Fine. But random_lowpass ifft with complex spec not Hermitian; result real from ifft of arbitrary complex can be complex? We take real; nonnegative after clip; OK but may be mostly positive? It might be random. Fine.

def score_and_sort_seeds(seeds):
    scored=[]
    for lab,a in seeds:
       a=normalize_sequence(a)
       sc=compute_score(a)
       scored.append((sc,a,lab))
    scored.sort(key=tuple_first)
    return scored
```

`select_seeds(scored, extra_labels, max_seeds=5)`:
```
def select_seeds(scored, priority_labels, max_seeds=5):
    selected=[]; seen=set()
    # top max_seeds
    for i in range(min(max_seeds,len(scored))):
       lab=scored[i][2]
       if lab not in seen:
          selected.append((scored[i][0],scored[i][1],lab)); seen.add(lab)
    for lab in priority_labels:
       if len(selected)>=max_seeds: break
       for i,item in enumerate(scored):
          if item[2]==lab and lab not in seen:
             selected.append(item); seen.add(lab); break
    return selected
```
Priority labels: ['parent','current_best','central','rev_central','extra']; plus maybe best low. We can pass.

```
def seed_fractions(count):
    arr=[]
    if count==0: return []
    weights=[]
    for i in range(count):
       weights.append(1.0/(i+1.0))
    s=sum(weights)
    return [w/s for w in weights]
```
No lambda.

    chosen=[]; seen=set()

       for item in scored:
          if item[2]==lab and lab not in seen:

But if sc slightly worse, might still be better basin. Could accept if `sc < best_sc + 0.001`? High risk. Use exact best for safety. But to preserve low-beta progress, if sc not worse, use res.x. Good.

Need recompute y from a_new for consistency? `y_for_next = y_from_sequence(a_new, fix)`? If using res.x, y may be at bounds; okay. If a_new from res.x, y=res.x. If exact best, y=best_y. But if fix index changed? We keep same fix index; if new a has different max, parameterization still valid but y relative to old fix; okay. Could choose new fix at max to keep y small, but then dimension order changes? Fix index can remain; no need. However if old fix becomes very small, y for others may become large positive, hitting bounds. Maybe better to after each beta set fix = argmax(best_a) and y from best_a. This can keep stable. The parameterization with fixed index can change between minimize calls; that's okay. For each beta, choose fix = argmax(current starting a). Then y. This maintains dynamic range. But if fix changes, low beta progress? We can do after each optimize beta: set `fix = int(np.argmax(a_current))` and `y = y_from_sequence(a_current, fix)`. Good. For next beta, maybe different fix.

So inside loop:
- At start beta, `a_start` from best_y? We have `a_cur` array. Set `fix = argmax(a_cur)`, y=y_from. Run minimize. After, evaluate. Set `a_cur = a_new if ... else best_a`. Then next iteration recompute fix from a_cur.
- `best_y` can be set to y for best_a (with its fix? but fix may change). Store only best_a; y reconstructed when needed.

Initial best_a = a0. For each beta:
```
a_start = best_a.copy()
fix = argmax(a_start)
y = y_from_sequence(a_start, fix)
run minimize from y.
a_new=sequence_from_y(res.x,fix)
sc=score(a_new)
if sc < best_sc:
   best_sc=sc; best_a=a_new; update
# For next, if a_new score <= best_sc + tolerance (where best_sc after update) choose a_new else best_a
a_cur = a_new if sc <= best_sc + 1e-10 else best_a
```
But if we update best, best_sc=sc and a_cur=a_new. If not, best_sc old, compare to best_sc+tol. Good.
Estimate time should be representative; use first beta, after computing y for first a. It may change with fix; okay.

`estimate_fun_time` can take a, fix, beta.

Now, if `res.status` indicates failure, still use res.x.

Now, can we avoid bounds to save? Bounds list and clip in objective; if y near bounds, gradient not accurate. Use bounds [-40,40]; y initially from a ratios up to 0 to maybe -40 for tiny. Good.

Now, potential SciPy L-BFGS-B with many variables and bounds may be slower than unbounded. Could use method='BFGS' unbounded with y centered? But need positivity. Softmax with fixed last and no bounds requires y within stable; we can clip inside but gradient inconsistent. Bounds okay.

Now, if n=16384, bounds list 16383, fine.

```

`self_conv`: N=next_pow2(2n-1) same; c length 2n-1. Good.

Now, one subtlety: `cross_lag(a,p)` for gradient of c = Σ p_k * 2 cross? Let's verify with small example. n=2, a=[a0,a1]. c=[a0^2,2a0a1,a1^2]. p=[p0,p1,p2]. grad wrt a0: 2p0 a0 + 2p1 a1. cross_lag(a,p) with ar=[a1,a0], conv ar*p:
indices:
m=0: a1 p0
m=1: a1 p1 + a0 p0
m=2: a1 p2 + a0 p1
m=3: a0 p2
Needed m=n-1=1..2: [a1 p1 + a0 p0, a1 p2 + a0 p1]. grad=2*that: [2(a0p0+a1p1), 2(a0p1+a1p2)] matches (for a0 second term p1 a1; for a1 grad p1 a0 + p2 a1). Good.

   best=_GLOBAL.get('best_seq')

       n_alt = 16384 if best_len < 16384 else 8192

```python
"""
Non-negative sequence optimizer for
    score(a) = 2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

Strategy:
- Use a stable softmax parameterization with one fixed coordinate to optimize
  sequences on the probability simplex.
- Smooth the maximum convolution coefficient by a (shifted) log-sum-exp and
  run multiple L-BFGS-B refinements with increasing smoothing inverse-temps.
- Seed from several structurally different families:
  * parent if available
  * central-binomial family related to (1-x)^(-1/2)
  * power-law/Beta-like densities
  * Fourier/mixture/random-smooth functions
- First do a low-resolution global search, then refine representative seeds at
  a larger length (about 12k), with a final alternate-length polish.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_OBJ_STATE = {}
_GLOBAL = {}


def tuple_first(item):
    return item[0]


def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def clean_sequence(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return x


def normalize_sequence(a):
    a = clean_sequence(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def resample_sequence(seq, n):
    seq = normalize_sequence(seq)
    m = seq.size
    if m == n:
        return seq
    if m == 1:
        return np.full(n, seq[0] / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, seq)
    return normalize_sequence(y)


def self_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([a[0] * a[0]], dtype=np.float64)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_pow2(m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    return np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:m]


def compute_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S < 1e-300:
        return np.inf
    c = self_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    cm = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cm):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * a.size * cm / (S * S))


def cross_lag(a, p):
    """
    For a in R^n and p in R^(2n-1), returns g[j] = sum_i a[i] p[i+j].
    This is the useful part of the transposed convolution operator.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    need = 2 * n - 1
    if p.size < need:
        p = np.concatenate([p, np.zeros(need - p.size, dtype=np.float64)])
    elif p.size > need:
        p = p[:need]

    N = next_pow2(need)
    ar = a[::-1]
    FA = np.fft.rfft(ar, N)
    FP = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(FA * FP, N)
    return conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def update_best_sequence(sc, seq, label=""):
    if seq is None:
        return
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return
    s = clean_sequence(seq)
    if s.size == 0:
        return
    if float(s.sum()) < 0.01:
        return
    cur = float(_GLOBAL.get("best_score", np.inf))
    if sc < cur - 1e-15:
        _GLOBAL["best_score"] = float(sc)
        _GLOBAL["best_seq"] = s.copy()
        print(f"  [update_best] {label}: {sc:.10f} (len={s.size})")


def softmax_from_y(y, fix):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=40.0, neginf=-40.0)
    y = np.clip(y, -80.0, 80.0)
    n = y.size + 1
    if fix < 0:
        fix = 0
    if fix >= n:
        fix = n - 1

    m = max(0.0, float(np.max(y)))
    e = np.exp(np.clip(y - m, -80.0, 0.0))
    wfix = np.exp(-m)
    denom = wfix + float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(denom) or denom <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)

    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if fix > 0:
        a[:fix] = e[:fix] / denom
    a[fix] = wfix / denom
    if fix + 1 < n:
        a[fix + 1:] = e[fix + 1:] / denom
    return a


def y_from_sequence(a, fix):
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    n = a.size
    if fix < 0:
        fix = 0
    if fix >= n:
        fix = n - 1
    afix = float(a[fix])
    if afix <= 1e-300:
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
        a = normalize_sequence(a)
        afix = float(a[fix])

    y = np.empty(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    denom = max(afix, 1e-300)
    if fix > 0:
        y[:fix] = np.log(np.maximum(a[:fix], 1e-300) / denom)
    if fix + 1 < n:
        y[fix:] = np.log(np.maximum(a[fix + 1:], 1e-300) / denom)
    return np.clip(y, -50.0, 50.0)


def lse_objective_params(y, beta, fix):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
    if y.size == 0:
        return 1e10, np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
        y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

    a = softmax_from_y(y, fix)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return 1e10, np.empty(y.size, dtype=np.float64)

    c = self_conv(a)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return 1e10, np.zeros(y.size, dtype=np.float64)

    if beta <= 1.0:
        p = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        val = cmax
    else:
        shifted = beta * (c - cmax)
        if np.any(~np.isfinite(shifted)):
            shifted = np.nan_to_num(shifted, nan=-100.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=-100.0)
        shifted -= float(shifted.max())
        exps = np.exp(shifted)
        s = float(exps.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
            p = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / c.size, dtype=np.float64)
            val = cmax
        else:
            p = exps / s
            val = cmax + float(np.log(s)) / beta

    grad_a = 2.0 * cross_lag(a, p)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_a)):
        grad_a = np.nan_to_num(grad_a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

    T = float(np.dot(grad_a, a))
    grad_y = np.empty(y.size, dtype=np.float64)
    if fix > 0:
        grad_y[:fix] = a[:fix] * (grad_a[:fix] - T)
    if fix + 1 < n:
        grad_y[fix:] = a[fix + 1:] * (grad_a[fix + 1:] - T)
    grad_y = np.nan_to_num(grad_y, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return float(val), grad_y


def objective_wrapper(x):
    beta = float(_OBJ_STATE.get("beta", 1.0))
    fix = int(_OBJ_STATE.get("fix", 0))
    return lse_objective_params(x, beta, fix)


def estimate_fun_time(y, fix, beta):
    old_beta = _OBJ_STATE.get("beta", beta)
    old_fix = _OBJ_STATE.get("fix", fix)
    _OBJ_STATE["beta"] = float(beta)
    _OBJ_STATE["fix"] = int(fix)
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    for _ in range(3):
        objective_wrapper(y)
    dt = (time.perf_counter() - t0) / 3.0
    _OBJ_STATE["beta"] = old_beta
    _OBJ_STATE["fix"] = old_fix
    if not np.isfinite(dt) or dt <= 1e-9:
        return 0.02
    return float(dt)


def optimize_log(a0, time_limit, label=""):
    a = normalize_sequence(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 2:
        return a, compute_score(a)

    label = str(label)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = compute_score(a)
    update_best_sequence(best_sc, best_a, label + ":init")

    beta_bases = (5.0, 20.0, 80.0, 300.0, 900.0)
    beta_schedule = []
    for b in beta_bases:
        beta = int(max(10.0, float(b) * float(n)))
        if beta not in beta_schedule:
            beta_schedule.append(beta)
    if not beta_schedule:
        return best_a, best_sc

    a_cur = best_a.copy()
    est = 0.02
    try:
        fix_est = int(np.argmax(a_cur))
        y_est = y_from_sequence(a_cur, fix_est)
        est = estimate_fun_time(y_est, fix_est, beta_schedule[0])
        est = max(1e-6, float(est))
    except Exception:
        est = 0.03

    print(f"    optimize {label}: n={n}, est_fun={est*1000:.2f} ms, betas={beta_schedule}")

    for beta in beta_schedule:
        now = time.monotonic()
        remaining = float(time_limit) - now
        if remaining < 20.0:
            break

        a_start = a_cur.copy()
        fix = int(np.argmax(a_start))
        y0 = y_from_sequence(a_start, fix)

        maxfun = int((remaining * 0.68) / (est * 1.55))
        maxfun = min(maxfun, 20000)
        maxfun = max(maxfun, 120)

        bounds = [(-40.0, 40.0)] * (n - 1)
        _OBJ_STATE["beta"] = float(beta)
        _OBJ_STATE["fix"] = int(fix)

        try:
            res = minimize(
                objective_wrapper,
                y0.copy(),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    "maxfun": maxfun,
                    "maxiter": max(60, maxfun),
                    "ftol": 1e-15,
                    "gtol": 1e-9,
                    "maxls": 20,
                    "disp": False,
                },
            )
            x_new = res.x
        except Exception:
            x_new = y0

        if x_new is None or hasattr(x_new, "size") is False:
            x_new = y0
        x_new = np.asarray(x_new, dtype=np.float64)
        if x_new.size != n - 1:
            x_new = y0

        a_new = softmax_from_y(x_new, fix)
        a_new = normalize_sequence(a_new)
        sc_new = compute_score(a_new)

        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_new
            best_a = a_new.copy()
            update_best_sequence(best_sc, best_a, label)

        # Keep the smoother trajectory if it is not materially worse; otherwise
        # revert to the best exact sequence.
        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new <= best_sc + 1e-9:
            a_cur = a_new.copy()
        else:
            a_cur = best_a.copy()

        now2 = time.monotonic()
        if now2 >= float(time_limit):
            break
        print(f"      {label} beta={beta}: exact={best_sc:.10f}, last={sc_new:.10f}, t={now2 - now:.1f}s")

    return best_a, best_sc


def central_seed(n, reverse=False):
    a = np.empty(max(1, int(n)), dtype=np.float64)
    m = a.size
    a[0] = 1.0
    for k in range(m - 1):
        a[k + 1] = a[k] * (2.0 * k + 1.0) / (2.0 * k + 2.0)
    if reverse:
        a = a[::-1].copy()
    return normalize_sequence(a)


def power_seed(n, el, er):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    x = np.linspace(1e-4, 1.0 - 1e-4, n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.power(x + 1e-7, float(el)) * np.power(1.0 - x + 1e-7, float(er)) + 1e-8
    return normalize_sequence(a)


def random_fourier_seed(n, rng, harm=4):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    vals = 0.7 + 0.12 * rng.uniform(size=n)
    harm = max(1, int(harm))
    for m in range(1, harm + 1):
        amp = rng.uniform(0.04, 0.22) / float(m)
        phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        if bool(rng.integers(0, 2)):
            vals += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * t + phase)
        else:
            vals += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * t + phase)
    vals = np.maximum(vals, 1e-4)
    return normalize_sequence(vals)


def random_mixture_seed(n, rng, K):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    K = max(1, int(K))
    centers = np.sort(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, K))
    widths = rng.uniform(0.008, 0.30, K)
    weights = rng.dirichlet(np.full(K, 0.6))
    a = np.full(n, 1e-4, dtype=np.float64)
    for c, w, sw in zip(centers, widths, weights):
        w = max(float(w), 1e-3)
        a += float(sw) * np.exp(-0.5 * ((t - float(c)) / w) ** 2)
    return normalize_sequence(a)


def random_lowpass_seed(n, rng):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    m = n // 2 + 1
    amp = np.exp(-3.0 * np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m))
    phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, m)
    spec = amp * np.exp(1j * phase)
    spec[0] = 1.0
    a = np.fft.irfft(spec, n)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0) + 0.02
    return normalize_sequence(a)


def make_seeds(n, rng, parent_seq=None, extra_seq=None):
    seeds = []

    if parent_seq is not None and len(parent_seq) > 0:
        seeds.append(("parent", resample_sequence(parent_seq, n)))

    if extra_seq is not None and len(extra_seq) > 0:
        seeds.append(("extra", resample_sequence(extra_seq, n)))

    best_seq = _GLOBAL.get("best_seq", None)
    if best_seq is not None and len(best_seq) > 0:
        seeds.append(("current_best", resample_sequence(best_seq, n)))

    seeds.append(("central", central_seed(n, False)))
    seeds.append(("rev_central", central_seed(n, True)))

    for el, er in [
        (0.0, 0.0),
        (0.5, 0.0),
        (0.0, 0.5),
        (0.5, 0.5),
        (1.0, 0.0),
        (0.0, 1.0),
        (-0.25, 0.0),
        (0.0, -0.25),
        (0.25, 0.25),
    ]:
        lab = "pow" + str(int(round(100 * el))) + "_" + str(int(round(100 * er)))
        seeds.append((lab, power_seed(n, el, er)))

    for i in range(8):
        harm = int(rng.integers(2, 7))
        seeds.append((f"fourier{i}", random_fourier_seed(n, rng, harm)))

    for K in [5, 10, 20, 40]:
        seeds.append((f"mix{K}", random_mixture_seed(n, rng, K)))

    for i in range(4):
        seeds.append((f"lowpass{i}", random_lowpass_seed(n, rng)))

    return seeds


def score_and_sort_seeds(seeds):
    scored = []
    for lab, a in seeds:
        a = normalize_sequence(a)
        sc = compute_score(a)
        scored.append((sc, a, lab))
    scored.sort(key=tuple_first)
    return scored


def choose_order(scored, priority_labels, max_seeds=5):
    chosen = []
    seen = set()

    for lab in priority_labels:
        if len(chosen) >= max_seeds:
            break
        for item in scored:
            if item[2] == lab and lab not in seen:
                chosen.append(item)
                seen.add(lab)
                break

    for item in scored:
        if len(chosen) >= max_seeds:
            break
        if item[2] not in seen:
            chosen.append(item)
            seen.add(item[2])

    return chosen


def seed_fractions(count):
    if count <= 0:
        return []
    weights = [1.0 / float(i + 1) for i in range(count)]
    s = float(sum(weights))
    return [w / s for w in weights]


def low_res_search(deadline, rng, parent_seq, max_time=240.0):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    end = min(float(deadline), t0 + float(max_time))
    if end - t0 < 60.0:
        bs = _GLOBAL.get("best_seq")
        if bs is not None:
            return bs
        return central_seed(256, False)

    n_low = 256
    seeds = make_seeds(n_low, rng, parent_seq=parent_seq, extra_seq=None)
    scored = score_and_sort_seeds(seeds)
    chosen = choose_order(scored, ["parent", "current_best", "central", "rev_central"], 4)

    best_low = chosen[0][1].copy() if len(chosen) > 0 else central_seed(n_low, False)
    best_low_sc = compute_score(best_low)
    if len(chosen) > 0:
        best_low_sc = min(best_low_sc, float(chosen[0][0]))

    fracs = seed_fractions(len(chosen))
    for i in range(len(chosen)):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= end:
            break
        remaining = end - now
        if remaining < 15.0:
            break
        total = float(sum(fracs[i:]))
        slot = remaining * (fracs[i] / total) * 0.9
        slot = min(slot, remaining * 0.45)
        limit = time.monotonic() + max(15.0, slot)
        a_opt, sc_opt = optimize_log(chosen[i][1], limit, label=f"low:{chosen[i][2]}")
        if np.isfinite(sc_opt) and sc_opt < best_low_sc:
            best_low_sc = sc_opt
            best_low = a_opt.copy()

    bs = _GLOBAL.get("best_seq")
    if bs is not None:
        bsc = _GLOBAL.get("best_score", np.inf)
        if np.isfinite(bsc) and bsc <= best_low_sc:
            return bs
    return best_low


def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400):
    t_start = time.perotonic() if hasattr(time, "perotonic") else time.monotonic()
    # Use monotonic explicitly; above protects only against typo, but real call:
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(120.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t_start + budget - 45.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(20240521)
    _GLOBAL.clear()

    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size >= 8:
            p = clean_sequence(p)
            if p.sum() > 1e-12:
                parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        ps = parent.copy()
        ps = clean_sequence(ps)
        psc = compute_score(ps)
        if np.isfinite(psc):
            update_best_sequence(psc, ps, "parent")
            print(f"Parent score: {psc:.10f}, len={ps.size}")
        else:
            parent = None

    print(f"Starting multi-basin optimizer, budget={budget:.0f}s")

    low_res = None
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 420.0:
        low_time = min(260.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.13)
        low_res = low_res_search(deadline, rng, parent, max_time=low_time)
        if low_res is not None:
            lsc = compute_score(low_res)
            update_best_sequence(lsc, low_res, "low_res")

    if budget < 900.0:
        n_main = 8192
    else:
        n_main = 12288

    print(f"Main length: {n_main}")
    seeds = make_seeds(n_main, rng, parent_seq=parent, extra_seq=low_res)
    scored = score_and_sort_seeds(seeds)
    for sc, _, lab in scored[:6]:
        print(f"    seed {lab}: {sc:.10f}")

    priority = ["extra", "central", "current_best", "parent", "rev_central"]
    chosen = choose_order(scored, priority, max_seeds=5)

    window_deadline = deadline - 150.0
    fracs = seed_fractions(len(chosen))

    for i in range(len(chosen)):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= window_deadline:
            break
        remaining = window_deadline - now
        if remaining < 45.0:
            break
        total = float(sum(fracs[i:]))
        slot = remaining * (fracs[i] / total) * 0.92
        slot = min(slot, remaining * 0.52)
        limit = time.monotonic() + max(30.0, slot)
        opt_seq, opt_sc = optimize_log(chosen[i][1], limit, label=f"main:{chosen[i][2]}")
        if np.isfinite(opt_sc):
            update_best_sequence(opt_sc, opt_seq, f"main_done:{chosen[i][2]}")

    # Alternate length polish. If best is shorter than 16384, try 16384.
    # If best is already longer, try 8192 as a safer/faster final candidate.
    alt_deadline = deadline - 25.0
    if time.monotonic() < alt_deadline:
        cur = _GLOBAL.get("best_seq")
        if cur is not None and len(cur) > 0:
            if time.monotonic() < deadline - 220.0:
                if len(cur) < 16384:
                    n_alt = 16384
                else:
                    n_alt = 8192
                a_alt = resample_sequence(cur, n_alt)
                alt_seq, alt_sc = optimize_log(a_alt, alt_deadline, label=f"alt_n={n_alt}")
                if np.isfinite(alt_sc):
                    update_best_sequence(alt_sc, alt_seq, f"alt_done_n={n_alt}")

    best_seq = _GLOBAL.get("best_seq")
    if best_seq is None or len(best_seq) == 0:
        best_seq = central_seed(2048, False)

    out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in best_seq]
    if len(out) == 0:
        out = [0.5] * 64
    s = sum(out)
    if s < 0.01:
        out = [0.5] * 64

    final_array = np.asarray(out, dtype=np.float64)
    internal_sc = compute_score(final_array)
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"FINAL internal score: {internal_sc:.10f}")
    print(f"FINAL length: {len(out)}")
    print(f"FINAL time: {elapsed:.1f}s / {budget:.0f}s")
    print("=" * 70)
    return out
```
